Sport Backpack MS
                     I am impressed with the strength and resilience of our
                     business in the first half of 2026 despite a challenging and
                     uncertain external environment. The management team
                     is taking significant actions to accelerate the long-term
                     growth trajectory of the Company through its key strategic
                     pillars of growth. The Board remained focused on its
                     responsibility to support management in navigating near-
                     term challenges while ensuring the Company remains firmly
                     positioned to create long-term shareholder value.

                     儘管外部環境充滿挑戰與不確定性，我們的業務於2026年上
                     半年展現出的實力與韌性，仍令本人印象深刻。管理團隊正採
                     取重大行動，透過關鍵的策略增長支柱加速推動本公司的長遠
                     增長軌跡。董事會繼續專注履行職責，支持管理層應對短期挑
                     戰，同時確保本公司維持穩固根基，從而為股東創造長遠價
                     值。

CORPORATE PROFILE
公司簡介
With a heritage dating back to 1910, Samsonite Group S.A. (together        新秀麗集團有限公司（連同其綜合附屬公司統稱為
with its consolidated subsidiaries, the “Company,” the “Group,” “our,”     「本公司」、「本集團」或「我們」）的歷史可追溯至
“us” or “we”) is the world’s best-known and largest travel luggage         1910年，是全球最著名、規模最大的行李箱公司，
company and a leader in global lifestyle bags. We own and operate a        並且是全球生活時尚箱包行業的翹楚。我們擁有及
portfolio of consumer-centric and iconic brands, led by Samsonite ,        經營以新秀麗 、TUMI 及American Tourister 為首並
TUMI , and American Tourister , that empower our customers’ journeys       以消費者為中心的標誌性品牌組合，該等品牌為我
with globally trusted, innovative and increasingly sustainable products.   們客戶的旅程提供全球信賴、創新及日益可持續發
Building on our long history of industry leadership, our vision is to      展的產品。憑藉我們歷史悠久的行業領導地位，我
create a path toward a more sustainable future for our industry.           們的願景是引領行業邁向更可持續發展的未來。



OUR CULTURE AND STRATEGY 我們的文化及策略
The principal characteristics of our culture are:                          我們文化的主要特點是：


•      Our long-standing guiding principle, the Golden Rule, to “do        •        我們一直以來的指導原則以「待人如己」為
       unto others as you would have them do unto you,” is a legacy                 黃金法則，這是我們創始人傳承下來的訓
       of our founder and guides relationships between us and our                   示，指導著我們和員工與客戶、供應商和其
       employees, customers, vendors, and other stakeholders.                       他持份者之間的關係。


•      Our management structure empowers our regional and                  •        我們的管理架構賦能予地區及國家層面的團
       country-level teams and fosters an entrepreneurial, innovative               隊，並促進以創業且創新的精神經營業務。
       approach to the business.

•      Our increasingly strong global coordination that allows us to       •        我們日益增強的全球協調能力，使我們能夠
       more effectively share best practices and capabilities across                更有效地在各市場之間分享最佳實踐與能
       our markets while continuing to empower local execution.                     力，同時持續賦能在地執行。


•      Our encouragement of efficient, timely decision making and          •        我們鼓勵高效、及時的決策和透明度，並將
       transparency and minimization of corporate bureaucracy.                      企業的官僚作風減至最低。


•      Our employees’ shared commitment to act lawfully, ethically         •        我們的員工共同承諾，將按照我們的行為守
       and responsibly in keeping with our code of conduct, which is                則合法、合道德和負責任地行事，該守則每
       communicated to employees throughout our Company annually.                   年都會向全公司的員工傳達。


•      Our focus on fostering a welcoming culture for our teams,           •        我們聚焦於為其包含不同國家及背景的團隊
       which represent many nationalities and backgrounds, creating                 營造包容的文化，打造一個支持其僱員並有
       a workplace environment and business culture that supports                   助於彼等共同成長及發展的工作環境與企業
       our employees and helps them to grow and thrive together.                    文化。


•      Our employees’ shared commitment to our Company’s “Our              •        我們的員工共同承諾推行我們的「負責任之
       Responsible Journey” sustainability initiatives.                             旅」可持續發展倡議。

OUR CULTURE AND STRATEGY
我們的文化及策略

We believe that our culture is critical to successfully executing our     我們認為我們的文化對成功執行策略至關重要，並
strategies and is well aligned with such strategies.                      與有關策略配合接軌。


Our key strategic priorities for growth are to:                           我們促進增長的關鍵策略重點是：


•      Amplify and elevate awareness of our iconic, consumer-centric      •     加強並提升旗下標誌性、以消費者為中心品
       brands;                                                                  牌的知名度；


•      Be the clear winner in digital to further support multi-channel    •     在數碼領域確立領先地位以進一步支持多渠
       growth;                                                                  道增長；


•      Seize whitespace opportunities in lifestyle bags; and              •     把握生活時尚箱包領域的發展機遇；及


•      Continue to win with products that resonate globally.              •     持續以全球共鳴的產品取勝。


As our business continues to grow, we also plan to leverage our           隨著業務持續擴張，我們亦計劃利用市場領導地
market leadership, platform and scale advantages to further enhance       位、平台及規模優勢，進一步提升營運表現並支持
our operating performance and support our strong margin profile           各品牌及地區強勁的利潤率表現。
across our brands and regions.

•      We expect the constant currency net sales growth rate in the       •     我們預期2026 年第三季度按不變匯率計算
       third quarter of 2026 to remain stable and in a similar range            的銷售淨額增長率將保持穩定，並維持在與

       continued progress on our key growth pillars which will enable           長支柱方面將持續取得進展，讓我們應對該
       us to navigate these pressures and continue to drive resilient           等壓力，並維持穩健的基本銷售淨額表現。
       underlying net sales.

       and ability to effectively navigate uncertain macroeconomic              作關係，以及透過我們的緩解措施有效應對

       actions, will continue to enable us to maintain our strong gross         將繼續讓我們於2026 年及以後維持強勁的
       margin profile in 2026 and beyond.                                       毛利率表現。


•      We are focused on investing in marketing to secure long-term       •     我們專注於投資營銷，以確保長遠的品牌
       brand growth opportunities. Therefore, we continue to expect             增長機遇。因此，我們繼續預期2026 年的
       2026 marketing spend to increase as a percentage of net sales            營銷開支佔銷售淨額的百分比將增加至約
       to approximately 6.5%.                                                   6.5%。


•      Relative to the second quarter of 2026 and excluding the benefit   •     相較於2026 年第二季度並撇除美國關稅退
       of U.S. tariff refunds, we expect adjusted EBITDA margin(1) to           款的收益，隨著我們踏入季節性銷售淨額較
       continue to improve over the remainder of the year ending                強的期間並持續採取行動減輕成本壓力，我
       December 31, 2026 as we enter seasonally stronger net sales              們預期經調整EBITDA利潤率 (1) 於截至2026
       periods and continue to take action to mitigate cost pressures.          年12月31日止年度的餘下時間將持續改善。

For the Six Months Ended June 30, 2026 and June 30, 2025 (Continued)                       截至2026年6月30日及2025年6月30日止六個月（續）
Notes 註釋
(1) Net sales were US$1,680.6 million for the six months ended June 30, 2026 compared to US$1,661.7 million for the six months ended June 30,
    2025, a year-over-year increase of 1.1%. Net sales decreased by 0.7% on a constant currency basis. Net sales results stated on a constant
    currency basis, a non-IFRS financial measure, are calculated by applying the average exchange rate of the period under comparison to
    current period local currency results.
    截至2026年6月30日止六個月，銷售淨額為1,680.6百萬美元，較截至2025年6月30日止六個月的1,661.7百萬美元按年增加1.1%。銷售淨額按不變匯
    率基準計算則減少0.7%。按不變匯率基準計算呈列的銷售淨額業績為非IFRS財務計量工具，是以當地貨幣呈列的本期間業績採用與其相比較期間的
    平均匯率計算所得。
(2) Adjusted net income, a non-IFRS financial measure, eliminates the effect of a number of costs, charges and credits and certain other
    non-cash charges, along with their respective tax effects, that impact our reported profit attributable to equity holders, which we believe helps
    to give securities analysts, investors and other interested parties a more complete understanding of our underlying financial performance.
    See “Management’s Discussion and Analysis of Financial Condition and Results of Operations – Adjusted Net Income” for a reconciliation from
    our profit attributable to equity holders to adjusted net income.
    經調整淨收入為非IFRS財務計量工具，其撇除影響我們呈報的股權持有人應佔溢利的多項成本、費用及貸項以及若干其他非現金費用（連同其各自的
    稅項影響）的影響，我們相信其有助證券分析員、投資者及其他利益相關方更全面了解我們的相關財務表現。有關我們股權持有人應佔溢利與經調整
    淨收入的對賬，請參閱「財務狀況及經營業績的管理層討論與分析 － 經調整淨收入」。
(3) Adjusted earnings before interest, taxes, depreciation and amortization of intangible assets (“adjusted EBITDA”), a non-IFRS financial
    measure, eliminates the effect of a number of costs, charges and credits and certain other non-cash charges. Adjusted EBITDA includes
    the lease interest and amortization expense under IFRS 16, Leases (“IFRS 16”) to account for operational rent expenses. We believe these
    measures provide additional information that is useful in gaining a more complete understanding of our operational performance and of the
    underlying trends of our business. See “Management’s Discussion and Analysis of Financial Condition and Results of Operations – Adjusted
    EBITDA” for a reconciliation from our profit for the period to adjusted EBITDA.
    未計利息、稅項、折舊及無形資產攤銷前的經調整盈利（「經調整EBITDA」）為非IFRS財務計量工具，其撇除多項成本、費用及貸項以及若干其他非現
    金費用的影響。經調整EBITDA包括IFRS第16號租賃（「IFRS第16號」）項下租賃利息及攤銷開支，以便計入經營租金開支。我們相信該等計量工具會
    提供更多資訊，有利於更全面了解我們的經營表現及業務的相關趨勢。有關我們的期內溢利與經調整EBITDA的對賬，請參閱「財務狀況及經營業績的
    管理層討論與分析 － 經調整EBITDA」。
(4) Adjusted EBITDA margin, a non-IFRS financial measure, is calculated by dividing adjusted EBITDA by net sales.
    經調整EBITDA利潤率為非IFRS財務計量工具，以經調整EBITDA除以銷售淨額計算所得。
(5) Adjusted free cash flow, a non-IFRS financial measure, is defined as net cash generated from operating activities less (i) purchases of
    property, plant and equipment and software (“total capital expenditures”) and (ii) principal payments on lease liabilities (each as set forth on
    the condensed consolidated statements of cash flows).
    經調整自由現金流量（一項非IFRS財務計量工具）定義為經營活動所得現金淨額減去(i)購置物業、廠房及設備以及採購軟件費用（「資本開支總額」）及
    (ii)租賃負債的本金付款（分別如簡明綜合現金流量表所載）。
(6) Adjusted basic and diluted earnings per share, both non-IFRS financial measures, are calculated by dividing adjusted net income by the
    weighted average number of shares used in the basic and diluted earnings per share calculations, respectively.
    經調整每股基本及攤薄盈利均為非IFRS財務計量工具，是以經調整淨收入分別除以每股基本及攤薄盈利計算所用的加權平均股份數目計算所得。

主席報告




Jerome Squire Griffith
Chairman 主席


This is my first letter to Samsonite Group’s shareholders, and I am       這是本人致新秀麗集團股東的首封信函。目前，管
pleased to be writing at a time when the management team is taking        理團隊正採取重大行動，透過關鍵的策略增長支柱
significant actions to accelerate the long-term growth trajectory of      加速推動本公司的長遠增長軌跡，本人很高興能在
the Company through its key strategic pillars of growth. I am also        這個重要時刻向各位致函。儘管外部環境充滿挑戰
impressed with the strength and resilience of our business in the first   與不確定性，我們的業務於2026年上半年展現出的
half of 2026 despite a challenging and uncertain external environment.    實力與韌性，仍令本人印象深刻。



      Samsonite Group today benefits from a portfolio                      AMERICAN TOURISTER
      of globally recognized iconic brands, a diversified
geographic footprint, significant multi-channel
penetration, and a highly cash-generative, asset-light
operating model. These attributes have been built over
many years, and the strong business model continues
to provide resilience through periods of uncertainty. The
Board remains supportive of management’s continued
investments in brand building, innovation, digital
capabilities, and category expansion.

今日的新秀麗集團受惠於享譽全球的標誌性品牌組合、多元化
的地域版圖、深度的多渠道滲透，以及能創造充裕現金流的輕
資產營運模式。這些特質乃經多年積累而成，而強勁的商業模
式也持續在不明朗時期展現韌性。董事會將繼續支持管理層在
建設品牌、創新、數碼化能力及擴展產品類別上持續投資。

Seasonality                                                                 季節性
Our net sales are subject to moderate seasonal fluctuations, due            我們的銷售淨額受到季節性波動的影響中等，主要
primarily to increased retail activity during the summer travel season      由於夏季旅遊季節以及假日旅遊及送禮季節零售活
and holiday travel and gifting seasons. Towards the end of spring and       動有所增加。春末夏初，我們銷售淨額呈現上升趨
the beginning of summer, our net sales tend to increase, reflecting         勢，反映出為夏季假日而購買旅遊相關產品。9 月
the purchase of travel-related products for the summer holidays.            至11月期間通常亦為批發買家比較活躍的期間，因
The period from September to November typically also represents a           為彼等會在年底節日送禮季節來臨前增加存貨。此
period of increased activity from wholesale buyers, as they increase        外，儘管批發活動通常於12月降低，但零售銷售通
inventories ahead of the year-end holiday gifting season. Furthermore,      常會因年底假日相關旅遊及禮品購買而上升。在該
while wholesale activity typically slows down in December, retail           等銷量高峰期，我們處理、生產及滿足客戶訂單的
sales typically increase as a result of year-end holiday-related travel     能力如遇任何干擾，均可能對我們的季度及年度經
and gift purchases. Any disruption in our ability to process, produce       營業績造成嚴重不利影響。
and fill customer orders during these periods of high sales volumes
could have a heightened adverse effect on our quarterly and annual
operating results.

Our working capital needs typically increase throughout our second          隨著我們平均存貨水平為滿足消費者需求增加而上
and third quarters as our average inventories increase to meet              升，我們的營運資金需要通常於第二季度及第三季
increased consumer demand. Our accounts receivable typically                度增加。由於批發渠道客戶在夏季旅遊及節日送禮

(1) The geographic location of our net sales generally reflects the country or territory from which our products were sold and does not
    necessarily indicate the country or territory in which our end customers were actually located.
    我們銷售淨額的地域位置分佈一般反映出售產品的國家或地區，並不一定為終端客戶實際所在的國家或地區。
(2) Results stated on a constant currency basis, a non-IFRS financial measure, are calculated by applying the average exchange rate of the period
    under comparison to current period local currency results. See “－Non-IFRS Financial Measures – Constant Currency Net Sales Growth.”
    按不變匯率基準計算呈列的業績為非IFRS財務計量工具，是以當地貨幣呈列的本期間業績採用與其相比較期間的平均匯率計算所得。請參閱「－ 非
    IFRS財務計量工具 － 按不變匯率基準計算的銷售淨額增長」。

財務狀況及經營業績的管理層討論與分析


Non-IFRS Financial Measures                                                非IFRS財務計量工具
In addition to our results determined in accordance with IFRS              除根據 IFRS 會計準則釐定我們的業績外，我們亦
Accounting Standards, we review certain non-IFRS financial measures,       會審視若干非 IFRS 財務計量工具，包括本節詳述
including constant currency net sales growth, adjusted EBITDA,             的按不變匯率基準計算的銷售淨額增長、經調整
adjusted EBITDA margin, adjusted net income, adjusted basic and            EBITDA、經調整EBITDA利潤率、經調整淨收入、
diluted earnings per share and adjusted free cash flow, as detailed        經調整每股基本及攤薄盈利以及經調整自由現金流
in this section, to evaluate our business, measure our performance,        量，以評估我們的業務、衡量我們的表現、識別影
identify trends affecting us, formulate business plans and make            響我們的趨勢、制定業務計劃及作出策略決策。
strategic decisions.

We believe that these non-IFRS financial measures, when used in            我們相信，當該等非 IFRS 財務計量工具與我們的
conjunction with our IFRS Accounting Standards financial information,      IFRS會計準則財務資料一同使用時，令投資者可將
allow investors to better evaluate our financial performance in            我們的財務表現與其他期間及我們行業內其他公司
comparison to other periods and to other companies in our industry.        對比，從而更好地評估我們的財務表現。然而，非
However, non-IFRS financial measures are not defined or recognized         IFRS財務計量工具並非根據IFRS會計準則進行定義
under IFRS Accounting Standards, are presented for supplemental            或確認，其呈列僅為補充資訊之目的，且不應被視
informational purposes only and should not be considered in isolation      為獨立於或依賴其代替根據IFRS會計準則所呈報的
or relied on as a substitute for financial information presented in        財務資料。我們呈列任何非IFRS財務計量工具，不
accordance with IFRS Accounting Standards. Our presentation of any         應被詮釋為推斷我們未來業績將不受不尋常或非經
non-IFRS financial measures should not be construed as an inference        常性項目的影響。我們所在行業內的其他公司可能
that our future results will be unaffected by unusual or non-recurring     以不同方式計算非IFRS財務計量工具，這可能限制
items. Other companies in our industry may calculate non-IFRS              其作為可比計量工具的有用性。
financial measures differently, which may limit their usefulness as
comparative measures.

Our non-IFRS financial measures have limitations as analytical tools       我們的非 IFRS 財務計量工具作為分析工具有其局
and should not be considered in isolation or as a substitute for an        限性，不應被視為獨立於或代替我們根據 IFRS 會
analysis of our results under IFRS Accounting Standards. Constant          計準則對業績進行的分析。按不變匯率基準計算的
currency net sales growth is limited as a metric to review our financial   銷售淨額增長作為檢討我們財務業績的指標有其局
results as it does not reflect the impacts of foreign currency on          限性，原因為其不會反映外幣對所呈報銷售淨額的
reported net sales. Some of the limitations of adjusted EBITDA and         影響。經調整EBITDA及經調整EBITDA利潤率的部
adjusted EBITDA margin include not capturing certain tax payments          分限制包括不會反映可能減少我們可用現金的若干
that may reduce cash available to us; not reflecting any cash capital      稅項付款；不會反映日後可能須置換的折舊及攤銷
expenditure requirements for the assets being depreciated and              資產的任何現金資本開支需求；不會反映我們營運
amortized that may have to be replaced in the future; not reflecting       資金需要的變動或現金需求；及不會反映利息開支
changes in, or cash requirements for, our working capital needs;           或償還利息或本金付款所需的現金需求。經調整淨
and not reflecting the interest expense, or the cash requirements          收入以及經調整每股基本及攤薄盈利的部分限制包
necessary to service interest or principal payments. Some of the           括不會反映影響我們所呈報的溢利的多項成本、費
limitations of adjusted net income and adjusted basic and diluted          用及貸項以及若干其他非現金費用（連同其各自的
earnings per share include not capturing the effect of a number of         稅項影響）的影響。經調整自由現金流量的部分限
costs, charges and credits and certain other non-cash charges, along       制包括其不會反映我們未來合約承擔或考慮若干現
with their respective tax effects, that impact our reported profit.        金需求，如債務償還需求，亦不反映我們於特定時
Some of the limitations of adjusted free cash flow include that it does    期現金結餘的增加或減少總額。由於該等及其他限
not reflect our future contractual commitments or consider certain         制，我們的非IFRS財務計量工具應與根據IFRS會計
cash requirements such as debt service requirements and does not           準則編製及呈報的可比財務計量工具一併考慮。
represent the total increase or decrease in our cash balance for a
given period. Because of these and other limitations, our non-IFRS
financial measures should be considered along with comparable
financial measures prepared and presented in accordance with IFRS
Accounting Standards.

of a number of costs, charges and credits and certain other non-cash        項成本、費用及貸項以及若干其他非現金費用的影
charges. Adjusted EBITDA is defined as profit for the period, adjusted      響。經調整EBITDA定義為期內溢利，並經調整以
to eliminate income tax expense, finance costs (excluding interest          撇除所得稅開支、財務費用（撇除租賃負債的利息
expense on lease liabilities), finance income, depreciation, amortization   開支）、財務收入、折舊、攤銷（撇除租賃使用權資
(excluding amortization of lease right-of-use assets), share-based          產攤銷）、以股份支付的薪酬開支、雲端ERP系統實
compensation expense, cloud-based ERP system implementation                 施成本及其他調整。經調整EBITDA利潤率（一項非
costs, and other adjustments. Adjusted EBITDA margin, a non-IFRS            IFRS財務計量工具）定義為經調整EBITDA除以銷售
financial measure, is defined as adjusted EBITDA divided by net sales.      淨額。我們相信經調整EBITDA及經調整EBITDA利
We believe adjusted EBITDA and adjusted EBITDA margin provide               潤率會提供更多資訊，有利於更全面了解我們的經
additional information that is useful in gaining a more complete            營表現及我們業務的相關趨勢。
understanding of our operational performance and of the underlying
trends of our business.

effect of a number of costs, charges and credits and certain other                        我們呈報的股權持有人應佔溢利的多項成本、費用

our reported profit attributable to equity holders, which we believe                      項影響）的影響，我們相信其有助證券分析員、投
helps to give securities analysts, investors and other interested                         資者及其他利益相關方更全面了解我們的相關財務

performance. Adjusted net income is defined as profit attributable                        並經調整以撇除計入財務費用的認沽期權之公允價

Adjusted Free Cash Flow                                                   經調整自由現金流量
We define adjusted free cash flow, a non-IFRS financial measure,          我們對經調整自由現金流量（一項非IFRS財務計量
as cash generated from operating activities, less (i) purchases of        工具）的定義為經營活動所得現金減去(i)購置物業、
property, plant and equipment and software and (ii) principal payments    廠房及設備以及採購軟件費用及 (ii) 租賃負債的本
on lease liabilities. We believe adjusted free cash flow provides         金付款。我們認為，經調整自由現金流量可有效提
helpful additional information regarding our liquidity and our ability    供有關我們流動資金及能否在不使用若干核心經營
to generate cash after excluding the use of cash from certain of our      活動之現金的情況下產生現金的更多資訊。經調整
core operating activities. Adjusted free cash flow does not represent     自由現金流量不代表可用作酌情支出的剩餘現金流
the residual cash flow available for discretionary expenditures since     量，原因為其並不包括若干強制性支出，且經調整
it excludes certain mandatory expenditures, and adjusted free cash        自由現金流量的計算可能有別於其他公司所使用類
flow may be calculated differently from, and therefore may not be         似命名的計量工具，因此未必可與之進行比較。
comparable to, similarly titled measures used by other companies.

Basis for Review Results                                                  審閱結果基礎
We conducted our reviews in accordance with auditing standards            我們根據適用於審閱中期財務資料的美利堅合眾
generally accepted in the United States of America (GAAS) applicable      國公認核數準則(GAAS)以及按照《國際審閱委聘準
to reviews of interim financial information and in accordance with        則》第2410 號由實體獨立核數師審閱中期財務資料
International Standards on Review Engagements 2410, Review of             進行審閱。審閱簡明綜合中期財務資料主要包括進
Interim Financial Information Performed by the Independent Auditor        行分析程序，並向負責財務及會計事宜之人士作出
of the Entity . A review of condensed consolidated interim financial      查詢。審閱簡明綜合中期財務資料的範圍遠較按照
information consists principally of applying analytical procedures and    GAAS及《國際審計準則》所進行之審核範圍小，而
making inquiries of persons responsible for financial and accounting      有關審核目的乃對整體財務資料發表意見，因此，
matters. A review of condensed consolidated interim financial             我們並不發表該等意見。我們須獨立於 貴公司，
information is substantially less in scope than an audit conducted in     並根據與審閱有關的相關道德規定遵守其他道德責
accordance with GAAS and the International Standards on Auditing,         任。我們相信審閱程序結果為我們的結論提供合理
the objective of which is an expression of an opinion regarding the       基礎。
financial information as a whole, and accordingly, we do not express
such an opinion. We are required to be independent of the Company
and to meet our other ethical responsibilities in accordance with the
relevant ethical requirements relating to our reviews. We believe that
the results of the review procedures provide a reasonable basis for our
conclusion.

(b)   Changes in Accounting Policies                                      (b)   會計政策的變動
      The IASB has issued the following new, revised and amended                IASB已頒佈若干新訂、經修訂及經修改的
      IFRS Accounting Standards. For the purpose of preparing the               IFRS會計準則。就編製於2026 年6 月30 日
      condensed consolidated interim financial information as of and            及截至該日止六個月的簡明綜合中期財務資
      for the six months ended June 30, 2026, the following standard            料而言，以下準則於本報告期間生效。
      became effective for the current reporting period.

      In May 2024, the IASB issued amendments to IFRS 9,                        於2024年5月，IASB頒發IFRS第9號金融工
      Financial Instruments (“IFRS 9”) and IFRS 7, Financial                    具（「IFRS第9號」）及IFRS第7號金融工具：
      Instruments: Disclosures (“IFRS 7”). The amendments relate                披露（「IFRS 第 7 號」）之修訂。該等修訂涉
      to settling financial liabilities using an electronic payment             及使用電子支付系統結算金融負債及評估金
      system and assessing contractual cash flow characteristics                融資產的合約現金流量特徵，包括與環境、
      of financial assets, including those with Environmental,                  社會及管治（「ESG」）相關的特徵。IASB亦
      Social, and Governance (ESG)-linked features. The IASB also               修訂有關指定為按公允價值計入其他綜合收
      amended disclosure requirements relating to investments                   益的股本工具投資的披露要求，並增加具有
      in equity instruments designated at fair value through other              或然特徵的金融工具的披露要求。該等修訂
      comprehensive income and added disclosure requirements                    已自2026 年1 月1 日或之後開始的年度報告
      for financial instruments with contingent features. The                   期間生效。該等修訂對簡明綜合中期財務資
      amendments became effective for annual reporting periods                  料並無重大影響。
      beginning on or after January 1, 2026. These amendments did
      not have a material impact on the condensed consolidated
      interim financial information.

(c)   New Standards and Interpretations Not Yet Adopted                   (c)   尚未採納的新準則及詮釋
      Certain new standards, amendments to standards and                        截至2026年6月30日止六個月，本公司可能
      interpretations that may be applicable to the Company are                 適用的若干新準則、準則修訂及詮釋尚未生
      not yet effective for the six months ended June 30, 2026, and             效，且編製該等簡明綜合中期財務資料時並
      have not been applied in preparing the condensed consolidated             無應用。
      interim financial information.

      In April 2024, the IASB issued IFRS 18, Presentation and                  於2024年4月，IASB頒發IFRS第18號財務
      Disclosure in Financial Statements (“IFRS 18”) to achieve                 報表的呈列及披露（「IFRS第18號」）以令同
      comparability of the financial performance of similar entities.           類實體的財務表現能互相比較。IFRS第18
      IFRS 18, which will replace IAS 1, Presentation of Financial              號將取代IAS第1 號財務報表的呈列 ，影響
      Statements , impacts the presentation of primary financial                主要財務報表及附註的呈列，主要是規定在
      statements and notes, mainly the consolidated statements of               綜合收益表中將經營、投資、融資、所得稅
      income where companies will be required to present separate               及已終止經營業務的收入及開支分類呈列，
      categories of income and expense for operating, investing,                並引入界定小計項目，包括經營溢利。
      financing, income tax and discontinued operations, and
      introduces defined subtotals, including operating profit.

      IFRS 18 will require entities to assess whether they have an              IFRS第18 號將要求實體評估其是否具有
      IFRS 18 defined specified main business activity. For those               IFRS第18 號界定的特定主要業務活動。對

      management-defined performance measures in a single note.                 隨著IFRS第18號頒佈，IAS第7號現金流量

5.   Seasonality of Operations                                             5.   業務季節性
     The Company’s net sales are subject to moderate seasonal                   本公司銷售淨額受到季節性波動的影響中
     fluctuations, due primarily to increased retail activity during the        等，主要由於夏季旅遊季節以及假日旅遊及
     summer travel season and holiday travel and gifting seasons.               送禮季節零售活動有所增加。春末夏初，本
     Towards the end of spring and the beginning of summer, the                 公司銷售淨額呈現上升趨勢，反映出為夏季
     Company’s net sales tend to increase, reflecting the purchase              假日而購買旅遊相關產品。9月至11月期間
     of travel-related products for the summer holidays. The period             通常亦為批發買家比較活躍的期間，因為彼
     from September to November typically also represents a period              等會在年底節日送禮季節來臨前增加存貨。
     of increased activity from wholesale buyers, as they increase              此外，儘管批發活動通常於 12 月降低，但
     inventories ahead of the year-end holiday gifting season.                  零售銷售通常會因年底假日相關旅遊及禮品
     Furthermore, while wholesale activity typically slows down in              購買而上升。
     December, retail sales typically increase as a result of year-end
     holiday-related travel and gift purchases.

     The Company’s working capital needs typically increase                     隨著本公司平均存貨水平為滿足消費者需求
     throughout the Company’s second and third quarters as average              增加而上升，本公司的營運資金需要通常於
     inventories increase to meet increased consumer demand. The                第二季度及第三季度增加。由於批發渠道客
     Company’s accounts receivable typically increases relative to its          戶在夏季旅遊及節日送禮季節來臨前儲備存
     net sales during these periods as wholesale channel customers              貨，相對於銷售淨額，本公司於該等期間的
     build their inventory in advance of the summer travel and                  應收賬款通常會增加。
     holiday gifting seasons.

     tangible and intangible assets with finite useful lives. If there

20. Financial Instruments                                                         20. 金融工具
(a)   Fair Value Versus Carrying Amounts                                          (a)   公允價值與賬面值的比較
      All financial assets and liabilities have fair values that                        所有金融資產及負債的公允價值與賬面值
      approximate carrying amounts.                                                     相若。


(b)   Fair Value of Financial Instruments                                         (b)   金融工具之公允價值
      Fair value is the price that would be received to sell an                         公允價值是於計量日市場參與者於有秩序交
      asset or paid to transfer a liability in an orderly transaction                   易中出售資產可收取或轉讓負債須支付的價
      between market participants at the measurement date.                              格。IFRS會計準則建立一套公允價值等級
      IFRS Accounting Standards establish a fair value hierarchy                        架構，該架構排列用以計量公允價值的估值
      that prioritizes the inputs to valuation techniques used to                       方法輸入數據的優先等級。該等級架構給予
      measure fair value. The hierarchy gives the highest priority to                   於活躍市場中相同資產或負債的未經調整報
      unadjusted quoted prices in active markets for identical assets                   價最高等級（第一級別計量），以及涉及重
      or liabilities (Level 1 measurements) and the lowest priority to                  大不可觀察輸入數據的計量最低等級（第三
      measurements involving significant unobservable inputs (Level                     級別計量）。公允價值等級架構的三個級別
      3 measurements). The three levels of the fair value hierarchy                     如下：
      are as follows:

      •          Level 1 inputs are quoted prices (unadjusted) in active                •       第一級別輸入數據為本公司有能力
                 markets for identical assets or liabilities that the                           於計量日取得的相同資產或負債於
                 Company has the ability to access at the measurement                           活躍市場的報價（未經調整）。
                 date.

      •          Level 2 inputs are inputs other than quoted prices                     •       第二級別輸入數據為不包括第一級
                 included within Level 1 that are observable for the asset                      別的報價的資產或負債的可直接或
                 or liability, either directly or indirectly.                                   間接觀察的輸入數據。


      •          Level 3 inputs are unobservable inputs for the asset or                •       第三級別輸入數據為資產或負債的
                 liability.                                                                     不可觀察輸入數據。

      entirety.

      Interest Rate Swaps                                                                   利率掉期
      The Company maintains interest rate swaps which are used                              本公司利用利率掉期以對沖浮息債務相關的
      to hedge interest rate risk associated with variable rate debt                        利率風險。由於利率掉期之公允價值主要根
      obligations. Since the interest rate swap fair values are based                       據市場數據確定的可觀察輸入數據（如利率
      predominantly on observable inputs, such as the interest                              收益曲線）計算，故被分類為公允價值等級
      yield curve, that are corroborated by market data, they are                           架構第二級別。
      categorized as Level 2 in the fair value hierarchy.

      Foreign Currency Forward Contracts                                                    遠期外匯合約
      Certain non-U.S. subsidiaries of the Company periodically enter                       本公司若干非美國附屬公司定期訂立與採購
      into forward contracts related to the purchase of inventory                           主要以美元結算的存貨有關的遠期合約，此
      denominated primarily in U.S. dollars which are designated as                         等合約旨在作為現金流量對沖。對沖有效性
      cash flow hedges. The hedging effectiveness was evaluated in                          乃根據IFRS第9號金融工具 評估。於2026年
      accordance with IFRS 9, Financial Instruments . The fair value of                     6 月30 日及2025 年12 月31 日，此等工具之
      these instruments was an asset of US$2.6 million and a liability                      公允價值分別為資產2.6百萬美元及負債1.5
      of US$1.5 million as of June 30, 2026 and December 31, 2025,                          百萬美元。
      respectively.

      Non-controlling Interest Put Options                                                  非控股權益認沽期權
      The following table shows the valuation technique used in                             下表呈列計量第三級別公允價值時採用的
      measuring the Level 3 fair value, as well as the significant                          估值方法，以及採用的重大不可觀察輸入
      unobservable inputs used:                                                             數據：

      faster-growing lifestyle bag categories, and strengthens the           快的生活時尚箱包類別的業務比重，並加強
      Company’s digital capabilities. This transaction aligns closely        本公司的數碼能力。此項交易與本公司的主

      to acquire 85% of the outstanding equity interests of BÉIS for         條款及條件，本公司同意以178.5 百萬美元

      adjustments for working capital, transaction expenses and              債務作出慣常調整）收購BÉIS的85%發行在
